### CLM-KAK-JP-E0021-001: Private restraint ends in an actual summons without a proved weekly breach

Miyagi decides after last week to summon Sendai only once weekly out of concern for prep-school study and to tolerate minor unpleasantness. Renewed wishes remain unperformed in the corridor, but after PE she sends the usual message despite repeated concern. This is a tension between self-restraint and actual initiative, not evidence of a newly shared restriction or a demonstrated excess over one call in the same week.

Strongest countercase and limit: No earlier summons this week is counted. She expects any arrival tomorrow rather than today, and no receipt, response, arrival, fee or exact message text is shown. Teacher distress, trace preoccupation, public unease and rumor can all contribute; no single motive is isolated.

Evidence: OBS-E0021-001; OBS-E0021-007; OBS-E0021-011; p1–12,106–110,143–147. [E0021 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0021_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0021-002: A vanished trace leaves a noticed behavior and a resisted interpretation

Maika notices Miyagi repeatedly touching her arm. Miyagi becomes aware of her hand at the former trace site, removes it and reports that Sendai's mark from last week faded before even two days. She dislikes how her behavior makes it seem she wanted the mark to remain, while a pause becomes visible to her friends and its private cause stays uncorrected.

Strongest countercase and limit: This is Miyagi's trace and retrospective testimony, not Sendai's separate trace, remedy efficacy or a general medical duration. No recollection of prior touching is not proof of absence; the resisted possible wish is not an uncomplicated conscious avowal or a meaningless habit.

Evidence: OBS-E0021-003; OBS-E0021-004; p42–71. [E0021 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0021_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0021-003: Accepted public noncontact coexists with discomfort at unequal information

Miyagi and Sendai briefly meet eyes and pass without greeting in accordance with their agreement. Miyagi says she has no dissatisfaction, yet feels uneasy. She later acknowledges limited personal sharing but dislikes hearing a new detail about Sendai through Maika. The gossip chain becomes more specific without becoming verified truth.

Strongest countercase and limit: No demand to abolish the convention, direct accusation to Sendai, exclusive information right, settled romantic self-definition or Sendai motive is disclosed. Maika's report establishes that Miyagi heard a rumor, not a confirmed confession, acceptance or relationship.

Evidence: OBS-E0021-006; OBS-E0021-007; OBS-E0021-008; OBS-E0021-009; OBS-E0021-010; p90–141. [E0021 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0021_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0021-004: Actual friendship offers company without settling the guarded dyadic investment

Maika and Ami provide ordinary companionship, notice Miyagi's behavior and draw her onward. Maika invites her to juku; Miyagi privately imagines that company improving on solitude, then imagines and immediately rejects attending Sendai's prep school. The eventual summons coexists with these distinct social possibilities rather than proving that the paid dyad supplies her only valued relation.

Strongest countercase and limit: Miyagi has no attendance plan, and her provisional reply is not enrollment or a shared future. Maika offers a teacher-based explanation which Miyagi leaves uncorrected; no friend is shown receiving the private cause. Actual company does not give them omniscience or disclose Sendai's own need.

Evidence: OBS-E0021-002; OBS-E0021-004; OBS-E0021-005; OBS-E0021-011; p14–40,59–88,143–147. [E0021 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0021_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0022-001: A kept accommodation reenacts payment without making every initiative an order

The next-day visit fulfills the accommodation Miyagi recognizes, and a fresh5000yen changes hands. Within that paid frame, an expressly noncommand arm request, a stronger sleeve request, an unissued imagined command and a final explicit textbook order have different forms and outcomes. The role persists through varied local initiatives rather than proving every act was commanded or automatically authorized.

Strongest countercase and limit: Sendai apologizes but Miyagi says the agreement was kept; no numeric weekly breach, refund or new rate is shown. Arm presentation, intervention and final seating are real responses, while actual textbook reading remains outside the cutoff.

Evidence: OBS-E0022-001; OBS-E0022-002; OBS-E0022-004; OBS-E0022-006; OBS-E0022-013; p1–17,34–47,64–81,176–188. [E0022 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0022_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0022-002: Vanished old traces do not prevent a new painful trace or settle permission

The older arm traces are now reported/seen gone, yet Miyagi imagines marking again and continues contact through several distinct responses. Initial pressure leaves no trace; later nail pressure elicits voiced pain, increases and ends in forcible hand removal, with thin nail traces subsequently visible. Absence of the old mark neither erases its significance nor authorizes new force.

Strongest countercase and limit: The initial arm presentation and hand release are genuine local actions. Nonwithdrawal is Miyagi's basis for continuing, not blanket assent; no exact Sendai fading interval, remedy efficacy, clinical injury extent or new-trace duration is established.

Evidence: OBS-E0022-004; OBS-E0022-005; OBS-E0022-006; OBS-E0022-007; OBS-E0022-008; OBS-E0022-009; OBS-E0022-010; OBS-E0022-011; p34–158. [E0022 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0022_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0022-003: Participant testimony and a jealousy label narrow some unknowns without settling the feeling

Sendai reports refusing the confession, gives lack of liking and time constraints, repeats lack of liking under a hypothetical, and offers priority to Miyagi. She then names jealousy. Miyagi denies requesting the reassurance, privately denies jealousy and identifies the unpleasantness of Maika knowing what she did not. The exchange improves Miyagi's access to Sendai's stated account while leaving competing interpretations of Miyagi's investment unresolved.

Strongest countercase and limit: This is heard participant testimony rather than an independently staged past confession or complete identity/date proof. Neither the voiced jealousy attribution nor Miyagi's denial is an omniscient verdict; no joint permanent exclusivity term, exclusive information entitlement or fully disclosed private explanation follows.

Evidence: OBS-E0022-007; OBS-E0022-009; OBS-E0022-010; OBS-E0022-011; p92–94,110–158. [E0022 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0022_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0022-004: A friend-like coping account accompanies an eroding purpose and a renewed command

Miyagi now privately explains that after the post-spring-break incident she recalls, she tried games and ordinary conversation as friend-like treatment so the lingering feeling might disappear. She judges that strategy strained, cannot talk like a friend now, and says the original orders-only purpose is being lost. Her new explicit order restores a form of interaction without resolving what she wants from Sendai.

Strongest countercase and limit: This is a later self-account with a stated period, not a first-occurrence ordinal, sole lifelong cause or explanation Sendai already received. Real Maika/Ami friendship and a private future outing remain distinct; the textbook is produced and Sendai sits, but neither reading/aloud voice nor emotional resolution is performed.

Evidence: OBS-E0022-003; OBS-E0022-011; OBS-E0022-012; OBS-E0022-013; p29–32,142–188. [E0022 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0022_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### E0022 forecasts, reconstruction and authority

All18forecasts/13historical attempts remain unchanged:6first-action failures,1exposure-qualified gift match and6ordinary-baseline ties, including the retained Sendai input-location defect. No fresh formal forecast/model trial was registered for this familiar encounter. A realized character expectation, priority offer or source-comparison match is not an analyst test. M0.13/S0.14 remain draft_noncurrent/UNTESTED,0independently validated current domains. Earlier failures/countercases/source errata and66excluded unquoted claim-index rows remain. No V1 or future release was opened. Original I1/all dependent duties remain active and unfinished. Both OFF source-evidence archives remain local only.

| CLM-KAK-JP-E0021-001.v2 / CLM-KAK-JP-E0021-002.v1 / CLM-KAK-JP-E0021-004.v1 | PRESERVE | No further payment/summons count, newly timed old mark, performed friend outing or juku enrollment. Prior arrival/payment resolution, before-two-days account and independent company stay exact. |

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0023-001: A soothing voice also becomes a wanted stopping voice

Miyagi audibly requests a lullaby substitute while privately fearing needless words and regret, criticizing the confession-triggered summons and losing the opportunity she imagines yesterday would have provided. Sendai actually reads aloud; later Miyagi explicitly wants the voice that negates and stops her. Sleep removes that response while her touch continues. The voice serves distinct comfort and restraint functions without making either the complete motive.

Strongest countercase and limit: Actual reading and deep sleep occur, but no passage or exact duration is disclosed. The call account is private and later, not a spoken jealousy admission; imagined customary objections are not current utterances, and their absence neither grants permission nor transfers responsibility.

Evidence: OBS-E0023-001; OBS-E0023-002; OBS-E0023-003; OBS-E0023-004; OBS-E0023-005; OBS-E0023-007; p1–68, 85–100. [E0023 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0023_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0023-002: Remembered satisfaction does not remove remembered pain

Present cheek and lip contact reactivates a distinct prior wound memory. Miyagi recalls warmth without pain relief and satisfaction in the obedient Sendai appearing displeased; the subsequent recalled bite ends that satisfaction and increases pain. The memory supplies a later own account of mixed valuation, not a current repetition, a simple soothing act or Sendai consent.

Strongest countercase and limit: The prior licking was actually instructed and performed in its earlier sequence; current memory does not disclose Sendai's full interior, medical severity or a universally preferred response. Current lip softness is compared with what Miyagi did not notice then, not proof that every detail or act is identical.

Evidence: OBS-E0023-006; OBS-E0023-007; p70–88. [E0023 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0023_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0023-003: Known mark limits, covert contact and denials leave a knowledge gap

Miyagi recalls specific no-kiss-mark locations before actually touching shoulder/strap and bringing lips and teeth below the ear while Sendai is represented asleep and nonresponsive. She wipes as though undoing the act, then denies doing anything and denies the erotic accusation after Sendai wakes. Her belief that Sendai should not know is explicitly qualified; the accusation does not disclose every perceived act. The reader retains an event history that neither denial nor presumed ignorance can erase.

Strongest countercase and limit: No visible new mark is inspected, the blouse is explicitly not unbuttoned, and a location-specific no-mark formulation is not silently expanded into a verbatim every-touch ban. None of those limits establishes current consent. No Sendai-own narration resolves exact waking time, knowledge, sincerity or complete intent.

Evidence: OBS-E0023-005; OBS-E0023-006; OBS-E0023-007; OBS-E0023-008; OBS-E0023-009; OBS-E0023-010; OBS-E0023-011; p64–166. [E0023 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0023_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0023-004: A failed continuing order closes with care for a finite-use object

Initial aloud reading is performed, but Sendai is later observed asleep and Miyagi explicitly rebukes failure of the instruction to continue reading. Heard sleep accounts and a later nearly-eight clock reading leave the onset and elapsed interval unknown. A folded textbook cover produces repeated apologies and Miyagi's expressed tolerance alongside careful fold-restoration. The one-year association concerns the textbook use context, not a newly agreed relationship expiry.

Strongest countercase and limit: Only the first kick 169 is explicitly light; 179 states another kick without a renewed force adjective, and neither gains harmlessness or permission. Apologies concern the textbook, not acknowledged covert contact; local object care proves no pristine repair, complete emotional repair, resumed reading or departure.

Evidence: OBS-E0023-002; OBS-E0023-003; OBS-E0023-004; OBS-E0023-005; OBS-E0023-011; OBS-E0023-012; OBS-E0023-013; p13–68, 147–200. [E0023 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0023_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### E0023 forecasts, reconstruction and authority

All18 forecasts/13 historical attempts remain unchanged: 6 first-action failures, 1exposure-qualified gift match and 6 ordinary-baseline ties, including Sendai input-location defect. No fresh formal prediction/model trial was issued for this familiar encounter; a source expectation, actual character instruction/failure or comparison match is not an analyst test. M0.13/S0.14 remain draft_noncurrent/UNTESTED, 0 validated current domains. Earlier failures/countercases/errata and 66 excluded unquoted index rows retained. No V1/E24 original is opened; original I1/all dependent stages remain active. Both OFFarchives local only.

Miyagi own WEB and Sendai observed WEB advance from E23 to E24. Own Sendai E20, observed Miyagi E20 and later LN10 adult/historical-extra clocks remain exact. The current private wrongness judgment concerns earlier sleeping contact and is not backdated into E23. A local release succeeds before a later explicit stop fails; actual interruption, reciprocal force and resistance retain their event order. The offered amends are unspecified; a performed bite's compensation label is disputed. Spoken mark-fading prediction, private broad erasure wish/authority defense and terminal relief keep distinct recipient gates. No new inspected mark/duration, home departure, meal, payment or comprehensive repair is shown.

The finite acquired-LN01 counterpart is separately accepted; its added awareness thoughts, visibility wording, breathing, changed stop/removal order, anatomy and annotations are excluded from these WEB formulations. The original WEB retains its own full sequence and paragraph evidence.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0024-001: Private recognition of wrongness coexists with an authority defense

Miyagi privately judges the earlier sleeping contact inappropriate, calls Sendai neither friend nor lover, imagines an awake ordered alternative and admits she does not well understand why she did that. After the current bite she privately invokes command authority and their kind of relationship to dismiss a right to complain. Recognition, a conditional defense and later self-justification coexist rather than supplying one settled account.

Strongest countercase and limit: The wrongness judgment and motive limit are later E24 disclosures about earlier contact, not thoughts backdated into E23. The conditional order is unissued, the broad authority theory is private, and Sendai actually resists and complains. No mutually accepted unlimited rule, transparent motive or agreed relationship category follows.

Evidence: OBS-E0024-003; OBS-E0024-004; OBS-E0024-009; OBS-E0024-010; OBS-E0024-011; OBS-E0024-012; p40–65,127–178. [E0024 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0024_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0024-002: A visible button action sharpens an unresolved knowledge problem

Sendai fastens a button, which Miyagi has not previously seen fastened in this room. Miyagi freezes, regrets the earlier act and questions whether Sendai noticed it. She later asks about retaliation with a private neck-contact referent, but expressly cannot tell whether Sendai genuinely does not understand. Apparent ignorance, amusement and the final comparison with Miyagi's conduct leave recipient knowledge unresolved.

Strongest countercase and limit: The first-seen statement is bounded to Miyagi's observation in this room. Sendai does not explicitly recount the concealed act; the retaliation question does not voice its whole private object. Neither Miyagi's should-not-know belief nor Sendai's nonspecific replies establishes complete knowledge, ignorance or revenge motive.

Evidence: OBS-E0024-003; OBS-E0024-004; OBS-E0024-005; OBS-E0024-009; OBS-E0024-012; p40–78,127–143,169–178. [E0024 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0024_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0024-003: Local compliance is followed by an ignored stop and reciprocal force

Sendai obeys the ankle-release demand, then continues different contact and expressly does not stop after a later strong immediate-stop demand. Miyagi's private incomplete earnestness coexists with literal objections and performed hand removal. Miyagi then grabs the arm before an answer, explicitly names an order and bites forcefully; Sendai presses her head, reports pain and protests. These are distinct local compliance, refusal, interruption and resistance events.

Strongest countercase and limit: The unissued licking instruction is not a violated actual order. Attempted skirt lifting is not automatically completed exposure. Ambivalence does not cancel spoken boundaries, and an arm command does not establish permission for every following act. No verified skin break, blood, clinical degree or universal obedience/refusal rule is supplied.

Evidence: OBS-E0024-005; OBS-E0024-006; OBS-E0024-007; OBS-E0024-008; OBS-E0024-009; OBS-E0024-010; OBS-E0024-012; p67–158,169–178. [E0024 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0024_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0024-004: Contested compensation ends in Miyagi's relief at familiar displeasure

Sendai's future textbook-amends offer remains unspecified while Miyagi predicts probable follow-through and minimizes the object's value. After biting, Miyagi calls it compensation; Sendai rejects unilateral settlement and repeats that it hurt. Miyagi adds punishment, speaks of quickly fading tooth marks and privately wishes everything she did would disappear. Sendai gets off the bed displeased; Miyagi calls it usual and feels relief.

Strongest countercase and limit: The earlier careful book handling remains real. A performed bite with a disputed label is not mutually accepted amends. Fast-fading speech is not a new inspected mark or measured disappearance, the broad wish has no exhaustively specified object, and familiar displeasure does not prove forgiveness, repaired boundaries, full Sendai knowledge, dinner or a home departure.

Evidence: OBS-E0024-001; OBS-E0024-002; OBS-E0024-003; OBS-E0024-010; OBS-E0024-011; OBS-E0024-012; p1–50,145–178. [E0024 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0024_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### E0024 forecasts, reconstruction and authority

All 18 forecasts/13 historical attempts remain unchanged: 6 first-action failures, 1 exposure-qualified gift match and 6 ordinary-baseline ties, with the Sendai input-location defect retained. Miyagi's probable-amends estimate and bad feeling are character expectations, not fresh analyst trials. No formal forecast/model test was issued for this familiar encounter. M0.13/S0.14 remain draft_noncurrent/UNTESTED with 0 validated current domains; all earlier failures, countercases, errata and 66 excluded unquoted claim-index rows remain. V1/E25 originals are unopened; full original I1 and every dependent architectural duty stay active. Both OFF archives remain local.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0025-001: New recipient conviction leaves a bounded historical knowledge problem

Sendai reports partial waking with unclear initial perception, then current confidence from Miyagi reactions that Miyagi lips touched her neck. This materially narrows the reader-recipient gap at the later own clock while leaving exact earlier awareness, full perceived acts and Miyagi purpose unresolved.

Strongest countercase and limit: Her identification has real evidential force, yet its present reaction-based route does not establish the first moment of certainty, all E24 knowledge or a complete revenge account. Current-entry memory, E24 bed fastening and current unfastening remain different events.

Evidence: OBS-E0025-002; OBS-E0025-004; OBS-E0025-006; OBS-E0025-008; p23–29,42–43,80–85,104. [E0025 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0025_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0025-002: Gray friendship, welcome and finite-horizon fear coexist

Sendai welcomes attachment and qualifiedly does not dislike the earlier act much, while fearing an unnamed change beyond friendship, preferring a gray degree of closeness and anticipating difficulty with next-year goodbye. Her unexplained incongruity preserves an unresolved own conflict rather than one transparently settled wish.

Strongest countercase and limit: Current attachment and nonaversion are important; they do not revoke the unasked character of the earlier act, create prior assent, a mutually positive category or fixed bilateral expiry. The WEB different/not-right wording stays distinct from novel revisions.

Evidence: OBS-E0025-006; OBS-E0025-007; p80–97. [E0025 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0025_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0025-003: Different-class homework changes labor without a shared educational future

Sendai own task account makes the recalled homework order more burdensome because assignments differ. Her private study/options argument includes limits. Miyagi new undecided, conditional and indifferent university reply adds heard information while Sendai imagined shared-prep/maximal-effort suggestions remain expressly unissued.

Strongest countercase and limit: Sendai says the task will finish soon; no current writing method or completed submission is shown. Qualified grade estimates, conditional attendance and private preferences do not prove institutions, applications, family causes, a common future or guaranteed educational outcomes.

Evidence: OBS-E0025-004; OBS-E0025-005; p48–79. [E0025 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0025_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0025-004: Deliberate disturbance ends in comfort at familiar refusal

Sendai knowingly maintains a hair-removal pretext, acknowledges intentionality, wants Miyagi troubled too and privately frames discomfort as deserved. Information-seeking receives refusals/nonanswers; being ignored is unpleasant. A topic change and refused loan then yield comfort in familiarity, suggesting a situated way of regulating affect through a familiar negative response.

Strongest countercase and limit: The deliberate-act acknowledgment concerns accuracy, not a general moral defense. Neither all earlier acts being revenge nor a mutually known regulation strategy follows. The eraser does not hit Miyagi; private let-off does not establish accepted compensation, a mutual forgiveness exchange or comprehensively repaired boundaries. No future stability or analyst forecast is validated.

Evidence: OBS-E0025-001; OBS-E0025-003; OBS-E0025-008; OBS-E0025-009; OBS-E0025-010; OBS-E0025-011; p1–21,30–40,99–154. [E0025 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0025_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0026-001: Peer agency and managed visibility coexist

Sendai’s reasonably enjoyable school life and particular peer help coexist with image management and differing Umina/Mariko educational priorities. Their observed choices and source-qualified histories supply independent social stakes rather than a uniform false-friend group or mere substitutes for Miyagi.

Strongest countercase and limit: Mariko recommendation desire is qualified, Sendai’s too-late judgment is not an outcome and no full peer interior is available. Image rationale does not explain every helpful act; no introduction, copying or completed outing is shown.

Evidence: OBS-E0026-002; OBS-E0026-003; OBS-E0026-004; OBS-E0026-005; OBS-E0026-011; OBS-E0026-012; p13–55,145–165. [E0026 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0026_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0026-002: A private fold history becomes publicly misrecognized

A persistent cover fold lets Sendai privately recognize Miyagi’s textbook and claim its making, while a vague spoken explanation withholds the basis from Umina. The reported loan and Umina’s uncorrected attribution of the fold to Miyagi transport the object’s privately known history into public misrecognition.

Strongest countercase and limit: The maker statement is later own evidence, not a retroactively witnessed back-pressure mechanism. The loan exchange and motive are unshown, no private arrangement is disclosed, and E25 requested physical book identity remains unestablished relative to this textbook.

Evidence: OBS-E0026-006; OBS-E0026-007; p57–95. [E0026 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0026_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0026-003: Selective affect without ownership or a settled diagnosis

Sendai expressly declines an ownership claim yet feels a cost when Umina uses Miyagi’s recognized book, compares Umina’s earlier name lapse with her own attention and avoids investigating the troublesome feeling. Selective salience and active noninquiry remain represented without a settled private diagnosis.

Strongest countercase and limit: Jealousy-like exclusivity, concealment risk and sensitivity to casual treatment remain rivals. Genuine peer life and no ownership are real; the teacher’s unpleasant voice and lead snap do not prove an intentional break or one known physical cause.

Evidence: OBS-E0026-007; OBS-E0026-008; OBS-E0026-009; p81–82,106–126. [E0026 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0026_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0026-004: Available initiative remains unexecuted beside actual peer departure

Sendai privately wants current contact, considers an advance visiting message and reasons that initiating is not prohibited, then stows the phone and restores her own expectation that Miyagi should initiate while leaving the classroom with friends. A recognized option, unvoiced role expectation and performed social action remain distinct.

Strongest countercase and limit: Earlier E16 texts/uncalled visit/E17 admission rule out universal passivity. No new bilateral grant/ban, current send/visit/receipt, introduction, destination arrival or complete hidden motive is shown; current contact wish does not erase explicit nonloneliness.

Evidence: OBS-E0026-010; OBS-E0026-011; OBS-E0026-012; p128–165. [E0026 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0026_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0027-001: Paid return carries situated conflict

Sendai’s payment-based timing rationale and emphatic nonconcern coexist with the dark approach, comparative attraction of Miyagi’s place and today-specific irritation. Actual paid arrival revises the earlier contact cutoff only at this later clock.

Strongest countercase and limit: Genuine E26 peer life, earlier actual initiatives and local familiar comfort remain. Neither a sole jealousy/delay cause, household diagnosis, universal dependency nor a conscious lie is proved.

Evidence: OBS-E0027-001–005; p1–52. [E0027 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0027_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0027-002: Private intimacy meets action-specific refusal

Sendai privately treats secrets and nonordinary acts as warrant for given-name intimacy, while Miyagi explicitly refuses after the name was already used. Feeding is refused twice although Miyagi chooses to eat for herself.

Strongest countercase and limit: No mutually accepted category, hidden assent, permanent naming pact or all-food rejection follows. Known-name disclosure does not date its acquisition, and the analyst’s earlier Miyagi-private explanation does not become Sendai’s knowledge.

Evidence: OBS-E0027-006–008; p54–112. [E0027 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0027_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0027-003: Own mirroring develops beside a failure to stop

Sendai initiates contact without a new command and deliberately continues after an explicit stop before forcible separation. Her own thrill, inferred mirroring and failed shallow-involvement account disclose a conflict in her self-understanding.

Strongest countercase and limit: E12 already contains private interest in displeasure and uncommanded initiative; E27 is no first-ever proof. No identical Miyagi interior, clinical injury, harmlessness, justified retaliation, mutual enjoyment or repair is established. Later local cooperation does not cancel the stop failure.

Evidence: OBS-E0027-009–012; p114–171; prior PROP-KAK-JP-E0012-012. [E0027 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0027_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0027-004: Paid obligation and a game remain prospective

Miyagi specifies eraser hiding in the uniform and later search; Sendai privately invokes paid obligation/minor over-clothes expectations while taking the eraser and issuing a preparatory turn request that Miyagi follows. The command frame contains task-specific agency on both sides.

Strongest countercase and limit: Private no-refusal reasoning supplies no objective waiver, continuing body access or guaranteed safeguard. Actual pickup/turn is preparatory only: no hiding, searching, subsequent touching, discovery, task completion or visit end is shown.

Evidence: OBS-E0027-013–014; p173–199/end. [E0027 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0027_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

The complete frozen WEB episode contains210native nodes:130meaningful and80format-only LF-br nodes, with0ruby/body media/replacements. Thirteen observations cover all130meaningful nodes once. Ordered original blocks, substantive firstnotes and whole Q1 precede current peer and finite-novel meanings. Source, material and finite components are accepted separately. These cumulative appendices have accepted component review; whole-transaction acceptance remains separate; they do not advance a canonical highwater by themselves.

This is current WEB reconciliation. SHARED means the pair's represented local speech/actions, not shared private reasons; PRIVATE remains Sendai/reader access; MIXED separates its action and private clauses; OWN_ACTION does not imply Miyagi witnessed hiding while turned away. Earlier reports, current acts, conditional threats and prospective possibilities retain distinct clocks. Grouped actor-event spans may overlap; observations retain the full interleaved order. Novel-only own appraisals, lexical contrasts and ruby stay in the separate finite correspondence.

OwnMiyagiE24, Sendai-observed-through-MiyagiE24 and all adult/historical LN10clocks remain. The location is finally confirmed, but eraser extraction/return and visit departure are not narrated. A requested clothes-removal restriction is explicitly unratified; existing sex/violence terms are not replaced. Private joke/shared-line accounts neither erase earlier actual objections nor certify complete shared intent or repair.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0028-001: Performed task and narrow ending

E28 carries the previously prepared eraser task through actual right-blazer hiding, a multi-stage search, shared location confirmation and an explicit two-party command ending. Its narrated endpoint remains post-command seated proximity, without eraser extraction, visit departure or settled relationship repair.

Strongest countercase and limit: Sendai’s earlier unilateral ending demands do not by themselves end the task; Miyagi’s final agreement does. Particular ongoing proximity is neither proof of full repair nor evidence that no limit was achieved.

Evidence: OBS-E0028-001,002,012,013; p14–21,187–210. [E0028 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0028_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0028-002: Variable limits and active agency

The search produces differentiated local outcomes: initial qualified tolerance, failed no-movement/ending requests and forced removals coexist with a conditional change of search site that Miyagi actually honors, later withdrawal, restored clothing and final closure. Sendai’s conditional threat, kick and footstep invoke the existing violence restriction without supplying unlimited permissions or a new standing rule.

Strongest countercase and limit: Actual specific compliance atp104 and withdrawal atp121 prevent a blanket never-stops account. Earlier refusals, continued unwanted conduct and achieved hand removals prevent an account of universal successful refusal or universal welcome; no moral offset between actors substitutes for sequence.

Evidence: OBS-E0028-002–007,010,012,013; p27–126,164–170,187–210. [E0028 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0028_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0028-003: Provocation, affect and unknown naming cause

Sendai knowingly reuses a refused name and reports relief at Miyagi’s displeasure, wishing Miyagi troubled too. That is current evidence of her own affective provocation beside resistance and uncertain sensory association, while Miyagi’s stated refusal and Sendai’s explicit ignorance leave the cause and Miyagi’s private experience open.

Strongest countercase and limit: Earlier E12interest/initiative and E27naming/contact block first-ever claims. Ordinary peer comparisons persist. The actual offered repeat is not a second performed name use, and neither displeasure nor Sendai’s inferred retaliation establishes an exhaustive mutual motive or positive relationship category.

Evidence: OBS-E0028-004,005,008,009,013; p50–53,73,85,137–154,206–207; earlier E12/E27 as countercase. [E0028 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0028_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0028-004: Private game explanations and unratified scope proposal

Sendai’s predetermined-loss, retaliation, endurance/shared-line and plea-waiting accounts remain private theories beside observable refusals and interventions. The later dialogue invokes the existing sex prohibition and explicitly requests a clothes-removal addition, but Miyagi’s joke/no-intention response does not explicitly ratify that new standing term.

Strongest countercase and limit: Miyagi’s checking and joking accounts are real spoken explanations, not independently proved full motives. Sendai’s professed joke knowledge coexists with prior actual pleas and a bad-joke judgment; the text supports neither conscious-lie certainty nor complete welcome. Existing no-sex/no-violence terms retain their original scope.

Evidence: OBS-E0028-001,004,006,010,011,013; p10–11,50–56,91–104,164–185,206–207. [E0028 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0028_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0029-001: Miyagi's solitary dinner exposes a shared habit

After Miyagi sends Sendai home, preparing two cups automatically preserves their visit-day dinner habit inside a solitary meal. The spacious common rooms, failed father-meal recall and poorer food make Miyagi's preference for company explicit, including even Sendai, while her blame account coexists with her own decision to send Sendai away.

No current shared dinner, exhaustive family cause, permanent father absence, matching Sendai feeling, friendship declaration or repaired earlier boundary follows. The former quick silent meal's incomplete companionship remains valid. Evidence: OBS-E0029-001,002,004,007; p1–31,39–47,78–83; prior complete meal/household records and their countercases retained.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0029-002: Miyagi's desire to touch follows an inferred invitation

Miyagi's own account adds naming shock, unexpected bodily disturbance and a sense that Sendai has moved too close. She interprets Sendai's touching as if inviting greater closeness and says her own wish to touch resulted in the eraser search. The private explanation expands reader access while preserving actual unwanted naming, uncommanded acts and earlier refusals.

The perceived invitation is not Sendai's permission utterance or blanket assent. It is not the exhaustive reason for naming aversion, a delivered explanation, a complete motive for the older sleeping-neck act or retrospectively welcomed noncompliance. Solo fingertip checking proves no mutual new contact. Evidence: OBS-E0029-003,005,007; p33–37,49–59,85–87; complete prior naming, uncommanded-contact and eraser-purpose records.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0029-003: Private authority reasoning leaves Miyagi awkward

Miyagi now thinks Sendai probably noticed the earlier textbook-day neck touch and attributes changed behavior to its aftermath. She answers that uncertainty with a broad within-rules command theory, including imagined correction and forgetting, yet still feels awkward as if she acted improperly. Her private self-authorization does not settle either Sendai's knowledge or her own felt limit.

No forgetting command is issued or shown effective. Earlier adverse sleeping-act judgment and site-specific limits remain; current as-if wording neither recants nor categorically confesses. Sendai's prior confidence is separately disclosed, and current behavior's sole cause and exact awareness time remain unknown. Evidence: OBS-E0029-006; p61–76; all retained neck-awareness, private-counterfactual and command-rule histories.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0029-004: The ended eraser task leaves a separate object consequence

E28's explicit command ending does not supply eraser return. Miyagi later reports nonreturn, links it to abandoned intended homework, imagines a different homework assignment and future Maika help, then actually buys a replacement. Separating act, counterfactual, plan and outcome keeps task completion distinct from recovery and interpersonal repair.

No current Sendai possession, loss, extraction, gift, delivered complaint, performed Maika help or completed homework is established. The old command stays ended; a replacement is not a return or acceptance communicated to Sendai. Evidence: OBS-E0029-001,008,009; p15,89–111; complete E27/E28 hiding, confirmation and task-closure histories.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0029-005: Miyagi resumes contact without a fresh errand

With the practical eraser obstacle replaced and no unpleasant event large enough to prompt a summons, Miyagi describes difficulty calling but finds continued silence untenable after a week from the last calling day. She privately explains that abruptly stopping would be strange and sends her first self-described message without an errand. A reply arrives promptly; Sendai reports preparatory school and actually comes to Miyagi's room the next day, consistent with the already established scheduling accommodation.

This is not first-ever contact or interest, zero possible annoyance, a new weekly minimum/ceiling, exact calendar, newly negotiated appointment or access to Sendai's new interior. School non-addressing, the attributed no-dissatisfaction avowal, older counterinitiative and lack of new category/repair remain. Evidence: OBS-E0029-009,010; p106–121/end; complete school/phone, contact-habit, private weekly restraint and E18/E22 next-day accommodation controls.

### E0029 forecasts, reconstruction and authority

All18 forecasts/13 historical attempts retain the six first-action failures, exposure-qualified gift match, six ordinary-baseline ties and Sendai input-location defect. M0.13/S0.14 remain draft_noncurrent/UNTESTED with zero validated domains. No new forecast, model fit, readiness promotion, V1 consultation or evaluator-owned write occurs. The complete frozen431/I1 and dependent G3–G7 duties continue after whole E29 and its own publication checks; no routine unit pause or full-goal completion follows.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0030-001: Familiar payment steadies a visit without an immediate task

Miyagi's no-errand summons and inability to choose an immediate instruction coexist with clothing-related unease and relief at Sendai's familiar payment acceptance. Miyagi gives a busy excuse that her own narration explicitly marks false, preserving a difference between received routine and reader access to her private account.

No first-ever initiative, absence of all motives, automatic Sendai recognition of the excuse, permanent weekly obligation, blanket paid permission or shared category follows. E29's different no-dissatisfaction avowal is not retroactively proved a conscious lie. Evidence: OBS-E0030-001,002,003; p1–49; prior actual contact, payment and scheduling histories must be retained.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0030-002: Self-occupation retains homework while useful help arrives unrequested

Miyagi keeps the homework to occupy herself against an unspecified feared action, rather than because she wants to study. Both work, Miyagi struggles, and Sendai later performs corrections that Miyagi finds clear despite the refusal of help and offered manga alternative. Study behavior, private reason, performed competence and interpersonal permission remain distinct.

No completed assignment, universal ability or diagnosis, specified feared act, exhaustive old-kiss motive or permanent homework rule is supplied. Useful explanation does not erase a delivered refusal, and a local refusal does not negate every earlier accepted or commanded help. Evidence: OBS-E0030-003,004,005,006,009; p32–113,157–161; earlier homework/counterinitiative and competence controls remain at their source clocks in the accepted material reconciliation.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0030-003: Performed distance and voiced limits coexist with explicit reapproach

Miyagi voices pain, refuses help, pushes shoulder and arm to create space, directs Sendai toward manga and reverses unilateral cooling. Sendai's useful instruction coexists with unrequested intervention and an explicitly renewed approach. Miyagi's actual general heat preference, only-heat avowal and accusation about Sendai's feeling have different evidential force.

An unspoken intended refusal at90–91 is not the delivered93 refusal. Created spacing is not voluntary compliance; later reapproach is explicit only at its own clause. No bare-skin contact from uniform closeness, proved Sendai thermal lie, blanket assent, total Miyagi passivity, global inability to refuse or adopted standing thermostat term follows. Evidence: OBS-E0030-005,006,007,008,009,010; p80–175; complete earlier site-specific refusals, successful accommodations and contrary actions must remain.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0030-004: A voiced neck-kiss question changes access while both reasons remain unanswered

Sendai's present nape contact leads to a specific question about why Miyagi kissed that site while Sendai was asleep. Miyagi now hears an awareness assertion beyond her earlier probability estimate, but responds by questioning why Sendai asks now. Sendai demands an answer first; the non-gentle ending supplies no answer to either motive question.

The sleeping frame and Miyagi's conditional counterquestion do not establish exact waking time, how awareness arose, full shared interpretation or a direct new Sendai-own motive. E29's search-related touch wish is not an exhaustive old-kiss explanation; earlier private naming/solitude/forgetting accounts are not automatically delivered. No fresh kiss, adopted answer-order rule or complete repair follows. Evidence: OBS-E0030-009,010,011; p153–181/end; exact prior own/observed neck-act, awareness and private motive histories remain separate.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0031-001: A warranted question meets a deliberate memory without a complete explanation

Miyagi privately acknowledges that Sendai has a right to ask and thinks she should answer, yet cannot consciously explain the earlier neck act. Her spoken mouth-contact/category defense and abnormal-conduct answer attempt coexist with remembered deliberate site contact and explicit uncertainty over Sendai's satisfaction. Public explanation, private memory and accessible motive remain distinct.

Strongest countercase and limit: No objective causelessness, definite hidden sole motive, amnesia, explicit source-labelled lie, complete shared explanation or new standing right/rule follows. A current answer attempt does not rewrite the unanswered E30 cutoff at its own clock.

Evidence: OBS-E0031-001,002,003; p1–55; exact earlier neck-act, private authority, awareness and motive histories must be recovered before formal adjudication.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0031-002: Conditional opening becomes particular contact and a prompted command

A held sleeve, desire for distance and location-dependent exchange coexist with Miyagi's private remembered lip touch and current wish to repeat it. Reaching becomes actual fingertip contact; Sendai's countergesture prompts withdrawal. Sendai then prompts an order that Miyagi resents being told to give, yet gives, and Sendai performs the eye-closing response. Initiative, permission inference and particular compliance remain locally differentiated.

Strongest countercase and limit: No blanket assent, absence of pressure, universal refusal failure, direct full Sendai-own intent, new shared standing term or new mouth-to-mouth kiss is established. Qualified intention receipt and remembered older acts must not become current shared knowledge or performed new events.

Evidence: OBS-E0031-002,003,004,005; p29–89; complete earlier site-specific limits, successful accommodations and counterinitiative controls remain required.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0031-003: Anticipated loss of visits interrupts a desired approach

Miyagi's attraction, actual approach and particular touches coexist with a no-transaction forgetting counterfactual, qualified ridicule fear and uncertainty about whether prospective contact is allowed. A conditional fear that a kiss might stop Sendai visiting this room accompanies the performed interruption. Local control of distance does not settle Miyagi's uncertainty about continued access.

Strongest countercase and limit: No actual future abandonment, solely financial Sendai motive, objective worth ranking, clinical/attachment diagnosis, exhaustive earlier-neck explanation or blanket Sendai permission/refusal follows. The112continuative wording remains provisionally typed;153explicitly confirms no completed current kiss.

Evidence: OBS-E0031-005,006,007,008,010; p88–127,152–154; paid initiation, different-class context and earlier company/avoidance controls retain their original event and recipient clocks.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0031-004: Expulsion creates a barrier while conversation and object business remain open

Miyagi apologizes, promises later contact and forcefully moves Sendai from room to entrance and outside despite protest and an attempted turn. The endpoint retains Sendai calling and knocking beyond the door, Miyagi's nonopening intention and usual-escort exception, confused current-kiss questions and the belated realization that the eraser question was forgotten. Entrance expulsion, continued local engagement and unresolved practical business coexist.

Strongest countercase and limit: No peaceful mutually settled exit, completed building/home journey, guaranteed future visit, full receipt of Miyagi's private reasons, precise unsupplied conversation topic, eraser return/location or reactivated search command follows. Current sounds are door-mediated, not a new direct bodily strike.

Evidence: OBS-E0031-007,008,009,010,011; p116–159/end; earlier ended command, unreturned eraser, replacement purchase, solitude and renewed-contact histories remain required controls.

### E0031 forecasts, reconstruction and authority

No new analyst forecast or test is registered or scored. Preserve all 18 forecasts, 13 historical attempts, failures/ties/exposures and input-location defect. M0.13/S0.14 remain draft_noncurrent/UNTESTED with zero validated domains. The prior source/material reviewer authored the retained private candidates; author QA is not independent self-acceptance. Separate independent review and root cumulative acceptance are complete. Novel-only comparison, complete-unit introduction and publication remain separate responsibilities. The full original I1 program and G3–G7 duties remain active.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0032-001: Corrected blame retains exploratory desire and a responsibility grievance

Sendai initially depicts the expulsion as unjust and herself as passive, then explicitly acknowledges inducing the eye-closing order to learn her own response to kissing Miyagi. She retains Miyagi's final choice and her own grievance over the interruption. Her present agency, privately stated motive and normative demand coexist without creating a shared duty to continue bodily contact.

Strongest countercase and limit: No completed current kiss, transmitted private confession, exhaustive romantic explanation, answer to Miyagi's older neck-act motive or newly adopted standing term. Private reproach is not an objective moral verdict.

Evidence: OBS-E0032-001; OBS-E0032-002; OBS-E0032-003; p1,2,4,5,7,8,10,12,13,15,17,18,20,21,23,24,26,27,29,31,33,35,36,38,40,41,42. Full source argument: [E0032 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0032_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0032-002: Money and avoidance predictions fail to settle lost access

Sendai's wish to return an incommensurate five thousand yen coexists with actual retention in the savings box, concern about future contact and a theory of Miyagi's avoidance. Four days without incoming Miyagi contact support the bounded predicted absence while leaving Miyagi's actual reason unreceived. Usual accompaniment, earlier comparable intervals, a remembered cider incident and unusual apology give different controls rather than a sole financial explanation.

Strongest countercase and limit: No actual refund, formal debt, permanent silence, elapsed one/two-week wait or directly certified Miyagi motive. The narrator's successful prediction is not an analyst model test; favorable-feeling expectations are not Miyagi-own evidence.

Evidence: OBS-E0032-002; OBS-E0032-004; OBS-E0032-005; p15,17,23,26,44,45,47,49,50,52,53,55,57,59,60,61,63,64,66,68,70,71,73,74. Full source argument: [E0032 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0032_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0032-003: Deliberate initiative meets channel-specific nonresponse

Sendai declines the peers' invitation while withholding her actual after-school plan, intends a first absence from cram school and visits Miyagi without a new summons. Repeated intercom calls, entry messages and a first telephone call produce no answer; initial read indications then cease. Her active choices and earlier successful admission counter a uniformly passive or universally denied account, while her private interpretations exceed what the communication statuses establish.

Strongest countercase and limit: No current residence admission, answered call, verified physical Miyagi location/intent, exact cumulative ring count, comprehensive private-motive transmission, actual parental punishment or general first-ever agency. Application marks do not certify cognition.

Evidence: OBS-E0032-005; OBS-E0032-006; OBS-E0032-007; OBS-E0032-008; p68,70,76,78,79,80,81,82,84,86,88,90,91,93,94,95,97,99,100,102,103,105,107,108,109,111,113,115,117,118,120,122,124,126,127. Full source argument: [E0032 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0032_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0032-004: Conditional ending leaves an opening and an unshared eraser location

Despite unstable feelings and study difficulty, Sendai minimizes a hypothetical earlier end, acknowledges regret and comfortable-place loss, yet refuses this unfinished closure. At her second home arrival, the thought that one opening suffices precedes moving Miyagi's eraser from the desk into her pencilcase. The action supplies new own-Sendai/reader object-location evidence and a possible contact-opening interpretation, without a specified future plan or Miyagi receipt.

Strongest countercase and limit: No actual mutual breakup, found replacement, achieved reconciliation, returned eraser, revived command, message about its location, full storage history or exhaustive motive. Private minimization does not cancel the conflict; narration/simile is not diagnosis.

Evidence: OBS-E0032-002; OBS-E0032-008; OBS-E0032-009; p27,127,129,130,132,133,134,136,138,139,140,142,143,144. Full source argument: [E0032 source and observation record](../03%20Web%20and%20Supplemental%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_KAK_JP_E0032_DEEP_READING.md#recoverable-observation-and-actor-clock-record).

### E0032 forecasts, reconstruction and authority

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0033-001: A public opening contests a privately acknowledged school boundary

Sendai creates school access through ordinary social contact and the eraser, while Miyagi invokes their existing promise not to address one another at school. Sendai's private admission of last-year acceptance qualifies her spoken unilateral-proposal argument; the classmate's friend label and the lost-property rationale create neither a mutual relationship category nor an adopted exception.

Strongest countercase and limit: Limited Umina disclosure, Miyagi's displeasure/quiet voice, Sendai's private sophistry admission and the distinct paid-command domain must remain. Continuing the conversation is not a waived school promise.

Evidence: OBS-E0033-001; OBS-E0033-002; OBS-E0033-003; OBS-E0033-004; OBS-E0033-005; OBS-E0033-006; OBS-E0033-008; p1,2,4,5,7,9,10,12,14,16,17,19,21,22,24,26,27,29,30,32,34,36,38,40,41,43,44,46,48,50,51,53,54,55,57,64,68,81,82,84,85,89,91,124,125,126. ### CLM-KAK-JP-E0033-002: Pressure and bounded independent movement retain separate force and permission

Sendai's relocation, repeated holding and command assertions coexist with Miyagi's protests, actual hand releases, self-walking and attempted departure. Local following or nonresistance does not adopt standing permission, while Miyagi's real agency cannot be erased by the surrounding pressure. A home-limited paid command right does not itself grant Sendai a reciprocal school right.

Strongest countercase and limit: No established injury, complete private motive, universal passivity, fully voluntary initial invitation or accepted new command term. Failed release130 and actual release144 remain different.

Miyagi gives a finished-business/eye-closure account and denies further implication; Sendai privately acknowledges inducing action while pressing for an answer, then transmits a study grievance to sustain the exchange. The real spoken answers, private self-critique and disputed causal blame preserve different access and explanatory scopes rather than a shared completed repair.

### CLM-KAK-JP-E0033-004: Forced return defers the offered gift while a message genuinely arrives

The eraser moves from Sendai's pencil case to her skirt pocket and then forcibly into Miyagi's hand. The early gift wording is Sendai's prediction; Miyagi actually offers it later, but Sendai defers receipt to Miyagi's home and leaves it behind. A message then genuinely arrives by the following lesson's end, without supplied contents or a completed home invitation, visit or reconciliation.

Strongest countercase and limit: No complete prior placement history, accepted re-gift, willing return receipt, known later object storage, quoted/read message, proved summons fulfillment or erased earlier nonresponse. The performed candy intake also remains.

Evidence: OBS-E0033-001; OBS-E0033-002; OBS-E0033-004; OBS-E0033-006; OBS-E0033-007; OBS-E0033-010; OBS-E0033-011; OBS-E0033-012; p4,5,19,21,22,46,48,50,51,53,57,59,94,96,97,98,154,155,156,157,158,160,161,162,164,166,167,169,171,172,174,175,177,179,180,182.
